def grad_a_from_weights(a,p):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-2; p_rev=p[::-1]
    term_rev = fast_convolve(a, p_rev) # length 3n-1
    term1 = term_rev[n-1 : 2*n-1][::-1]
    p_cdf = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(p)))
    s = p_cdf[n:2*n] - p_cdf[:n]
    return term1 + a*s
```

Let's consider **exact active-set Newton**. We can locate active peaks (top K). The objective max c_k is piecewise quadratic. At a local optimum with all a_i>0 and active set K, there exist λ_k for active c_k=M such that:

This linear stationarity iteration may be very powerful. Let's call it **Newton-free spectral balancing**:
Algorithm:
1. Given a, compute c=a*a, M=max.
2. Compute weights p = softmax(temp * (c - M? maybe c/M -1)?). To resemble KKT, p should allocate to active peaks. Use `p = softmax(temp * c)` or top K. For exact max, p at active set. Temperature large.
3. Build A_{i,j}=p_{i+j}. Solve A x = 1. If x has positive entries, set a_new = x / sum(x). If some negative, clip and renormalize or blend with old.
4. Evaluate score; line search between old and new (or accept if better).
5. Repeat.

This solves the stationarity condition for the weighted smooth objective (or active max). It is analogous to iterative reweighted least squares / PCG. It may converge to local minima faster than multiplicative.

We can implement a **damped stationarity update**:
- Compute p = softmax(temp * (c/M -1)) or `softmax(temp*c)`. Use c/M for scale.
- Build A and solve A_reg x = 1 (or maybe A x = 1) with regularization `A + μI` where μ small (1e-6 or based on norm). Normalize x. If x has negative fractions, blend with previous or clip. Since all desired a≥0, if x has too many negative, use `x_pos = np.maximum(x,0); if sum>0 use else clip`.

Given a0>0, the constraints c_k≤M for k=0..n-1 impose upper bounds on a_k depending on previous a_i (from left). Specifically for k<n:

If we want maximize sum, we can choose each a_k as large as possible while satisfying c_k=M and previous constraints? Since a_k appears only in future constraints; to maximize total under all constraints, one might set early coefficients to their maximum feasible following some strategy. Let's write `multiplicative_optimize` with correct gradient and line search.
Details:
```
def multiplicative_optimize(a, budget, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=normalize(a); best=a; bestsc=local_score(a)
    t0=now; deadline=t0+budget
    temp=30.0 # for weights? top fraction maybe
    K=max(4, a.size//16)
    eta=0.3
    stuck=0
    while now < deadline:
        c=conv(a,a); M=max(c)
        # weights: p = softmax((c/M-1)*temp) if M>0 else uniform over L
        if M>0:
           u=c/M
           vals=temp*(u-1.0)
           # clip vals to [-30,0]? since u<=1, vals<=0. exp underflow.
           e=np.exp(np.clip(vals,-50,0)); p=e/e.sum()
        else: p uniform
        h=grad_from_weights(a,p)
        # If h all zero? skip
        scale=max(h)
        if scale>0:
           d=(np.mean(h)-h)/scale
           # Add tiny random asymmetry if deterministic and no improvement?
           if stuck>20 and rng.random()<0.2:
               d += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
           # line search
           best_cand=best; best_sc=bestsc
           for step in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.2]:
               cand=best * np.exp(step*d) # use best not a?
               cand=normalize(cand)
               sc=local_score(cand)
               if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand
           if best_sc < bestsc:
               best=best_cand; bestsc=best_sc; a=best; stuck=0
               # slightly increase temp? if improving maybe temp*=1.05, K adjust
               if temp<200: temp=min(200,temp*1.05)
               if eta<1.0: eta=min(1.0,eta*1.02)
           else:
               stuck+=1
               a=best # restart from best
               if stuck%20==0:
                   temp=max(5,temp*0.7)
                   eta=max(0.05,eta*0.7)
                   K=max(2,int(K*0.8)) or increase?
               if stuck>80:
                   # inject noise
                   cand=best*np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)); normalize; if sc<bestsc accept
                   stuck=0
    return best,bestsc
```
Use `a` and `best`: After a failed step, set a=best for next. For line search, starting from best each time may be robust but slower; previous used a current chain. We can keep chain: after computational failure, a=best. If some step not improve but a changed, maybe try smaller. Simpler use best as base for each line search; gradient computed on base. This is projected gradient. Could be okay.

Could also try step 2? but may zero.
If score improves, update temp based on active set? To reduce max, we might want sharper p (larger temp) as peaks localize. But too sharp may get stuck. Use adaptive: if improvement >1e-5, temp=min(500,temp*1.1); else temp=max(5,temp*0.8).

`fast_convolve` for p length 2n-1 and a length n; for multiplicative we call many times. Direct `np.convolve` for n=512 is fine. Use `np.convolve` to avoid FFT overhead/roundoff. For n>4096 maybe FFT. We'll define:
```
def fast_convolve(u,v):
    if len(u)==0 or len(v)==0: return np.array([])
    if HAVE_FFT and (len(u)*len(v) > 2_000_000):
        try:
            res=fftconvolve(u,v,mode='full')
            res[np.abs(res)<1e-14*np.max(np.abs(res))] = 0
            return res
        except: pass
    return np.convolve(u,v)
```
But for gradient many calls, threshold high; direct C fast. Use direct for n≤4096 perhaps. `len(u)*len(v)` ~ n*2n=2n^2. For n=2048 ->8M, direct maybe okay; FFT maybe faster. Set threshold 3M and use FFT if available. But FFT on small arrays overhead. We'll use threshold 5_000_000.

def softmax_log(c, temp):
    x=temp*c
    m=float(np.max(x))
    if not finite: return inf, zeros
    expx=np.exp(x-m)
    s=expp.sum()
    if s<=0: ...
    p=expp/s
    return (m+log(s))/temp, p
```

Now `stationarity_solve_update(a, beta, reg)`:
- n=a.size; c=conv; M=max c; u=c/M; p=exp(beta*(u-1)); p/=sum.
- Build A: `idx = (np.arange(n)[:,None] + np.arange(n)[None,:])`; A=p[idx]. For memory, if n>800 skip.

- Solve via `np.linalg.solve`. If LinAlgError, use `np.linalg.lstsq`.
- x = solution. Since A may be indefinite, x may negative. We can take `x = np.clip(x,0,None)` if negative fraction <0.5; else try abs? If sum positive, normalize. If many negative, return None.

         g = lp_min_t(best, seed+it, timeout=20)
      else:
         g = lp_max_sum(best, seed+it, B=20, timeout=20)

      # line search mix
      improved=False

      if improve: best,sc; stall=0; improved=True
      else:
         # try small multiplicative 2 steps

         if improve update else stall++
      it+=1

```

Implement `smooth_value(z)`:
```
def smooth_value(z):
    temp = SMOOTH_TEMP
    n = len(z)
    full = np.empty(n)
    full[0]=0.0
    if n>1: full[1:]=z
    m = full.max()
    e = np.exp(full-m)
    a = e/e.sum()
    c = fast_convolve(a,a)
    soft,p = softmax_log(c,temp)
    val=2.0*n*soft
    h = grad_from_weights(a,p)
    grad_a = 4.0*n*h
    g = grad_a - a*np.dot(a, grad_a)
    return val,g
```
Wait derivative factor: val=2n*soft. dsoft/da=2h. dval/da=4n h. Correct.
But `h` computed with p sum1. If p from softmax_log(c,temp). Yes.
If val not finite, return inf, zeros.
`smooth_fun(z)` returns smooth_value(z)[0]. It will recompute. `smooth_jac` similarly.

In `grad_from_weights`, ensure p is 1D float length 2n-1. If p sum not exactly1, normalize.
```
def grad_from_weights(a,p):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if p.size != 2*n-1:
       # pad/trunc
    p = np.asarray(p,float)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    p=p/s
    p_rev=p[::-1]
    conv = fast_convolve(a,p_rev)
    # conv length should 3n-1. If FFT length maybe? full exact.
    start=n-1
    end=2*n-1
    if conv.size < end: # fallback direct
       conv=np.convolve(a,p_rev)
    term=conv[start:end][::-1]
    return term
```
No row sum. Good.

Test derivative with n=2: p center? Should work.

Multiplicative line search function:
```
def try_mix(base, target, t_list):
    best=base; bestsc=local_score(base)
    for t in t_list:
       cand=(1-t)*base + t*target
       cand=normalize(cand)
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=local_score(cand)
       if sc<bestsc: best=cand; bestsc=sc
    return best,bestsc
```
For multiplicative update target is exponential change, not linear mix. We'll implement specific.

`multiplicative_update_once(a, temp, K, eta, rng)`:
- compute weights (softmax or top) -> p
- h=grad_from_weights
- hmax=max(h)
- if hmax<=0 return a, sc
- d=(np.mean(h)-h)/hmax
- if rng and random: d += small
- best=a; bestsc=local_score(a)
- steps = [0.03,0.07,0.15,0.3,0.55,0.9]
- for step: cand=np.exp(step*d)*a; normalize; sc; update.
- return best,bestsc
If no improvement? returns best maybe same.
Use `a` as base (best). If we call repeatedly with same a after failure, no progress; need perturb.

`multiplicative_optimize(a,budget,seed)`:
```
rng=default_rng(seed); best=a; bestsc=local_score(a); temp=20; eta=1.0; K=max(4,n//20); stuck=0; it=0
while now<deadline:
   top = (it%3==0) and temp<100? Maybe alternate.
   cand,sc = update_once(best, temp, K, eta, rng, top=(it%2==1))
   if sc<bestsc*(1-1e-7):
       best=cand; bestsc=sc; stuck=0
       if temp<300: temp*=1.03
       if K<n//4: K+=1? maybe
   else:
       stuck+=1
       if stuck%10==0:
           temp=max(5,temp*0.8); K=max(2,int(K*0.8)); 
       if stuck>60:
           # random log perturbation
           cand=best*np.exp(0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)); norm; sc
           if sc<bestsc: update
           else:
              cand=best + 0.1*(np.ones(n)/n - best); norm; sc...
           temp=30; K=max(4,n//20); stuck=0
   it+=1
```

Weight function:
```
def peak_weights(c, temp, K=None, mode='softmax'):
    M=c.max()
    L=c.size
    if M<=0: p=zeros; p[argmax?]=1
    else:
       if mode=='top':
          k=min(L,K if K else 1)
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
          p=np.zeros(L); p[idx]=1/k; return p
       u=c/M
       vals=temp*(u-1.0)
       # vals <=0; clip lower to -50
       e=np.exp(np.clip(vals,-50,0.0))
       s=e.sum()
       if s<=0: fallback top1
       return e/s
```
For top K, if K None, choose `max(1, int(L*0.03))`.
Maybe use a hybrid: top weights scaled by softmax? no.

def quick_candidates(a):
    yield a

    n=a.size; u=np.ones(n)/n

    # tilt endpoints: w=exp(α*(i/(n-1)-0.5))

`lp_min_t` dense method:
```
def lp_min_t(a, seed, timeout=None):
   n=a.size
   if n>1024: return None
   # ensure positive? If a has zeros, F columns with zeros okay. LP may have unbounded t? no.
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n+1))
   for i in range(n):
       A[i:i+n, i] = a
   A[:,n] = -1.0
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0; c[:n] += 1e-9*rng.uniform(-1,1,n)
   Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
   b=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*(n+1)
   options={'presolve':True}
   if timeout is not None and timeout>1: options['time_limit']=timeout
   res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=[1.0],bounds=bounds,method='highs',options=options)
   if res.success:
      g=np.asarray(res.x[:n]); g=np.clip(g,0,None); g=normalize(g); return g
   return None
```
Potential memory: A for n=1024 2047*1025 ~16.8MB; okay. For 1024, loop 1024 slice assignments each length 1024, fine. For timeout, if not recognized maybe options key accepted? If error, fallback.
`lp_max_sum`:
```
def lp_max_sum(a, B=20, seed, timeout=None):
   n=a.size; if n>1024 return None
   F=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   for i in range(n): F[i:i+n,i]=a
   c=-np.ones(n); c += 1e-9*rand
   M=max(fast_convolve(a,a)); tau=M*(1+1e-7)
   b=np.full(2*n-1, tau)
   bounds=[(0.0,B)]*n
   res=linprog(c,A_ub=F,b_ub=b,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options=options)
   if success:
      g=res.x; gsum=sum(g); if gsum <=0 return None; normalize
```
But maximizing sum with B might optimize g entries at B; if B too large, normalized g could be very spiky; line search protects. Use B=10 maybe. Since a sum1, M~1/n, feasible g with values >1? If a positive, row constraints near limits. B=10 enough.

To speed, we can cache F? F depends on a every call; no.

def smooth_optimize(a,budget,seed):
   if not HAVE_MINIMIZE: return
   a_pref=normalize(a)
   a_pos=np.clip(a_pref,1e-10,None); if min<=0? add 1e-12; normalize

   temps = [10,30,100,300,1000,3000]
   if a.size>1024: temps = [10,50,200,800]
   for temp in temps:
      rem=...

      SMOOTH_TEMP=temp
      maxiter = max(5, min(40, int(rem/8)))
      try:
         res=minimize(smooth_fun, z, jac=smooth_jac, method='L-BFGS-B',
                      options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'maxfun':maxiter*4, 'gtol':1e-4, 'iprint':-1})
         z=res.x
      except: break
      cand=softmax_z(z)
      # try mix between best and cand

      if sc<bestsc: best=cand2; bestsc=sc
   return best,bestsc
```

def softmax_z(z):

Let's think about `local_score performance`: It calls `fast_convolve(a,a)`. If a is normalized sum1, c sum ~1. For n=2048, direct convolve 4M? Actually 2048*4095 ~8.4M. Good. For 4096 ~33M. Use FFT if product threshold. Need import numpy only? We import scipy.

Potential issue: `np.convolve` for length 4096 and 8191 maybe uses direct O(33M) but in C; okay. `fftconvolve` maybe returns length 12287 and uses rfft; faster. We'll enable FFT for product > 4_000_000.

Implement `fast_convolve` robust:
```
def fast_convolve(u,v):
    u=np.asarray(u,float); v=np.asarray(v,float)
    if u.size==0 or v.size==0: return np.array([],float)
    if HAVE_FFT and u.size*v.size > 4000000:
       try:
          res=fftconvolve(u,v,mode='full')
          res=np.asarray(res,float)
          maxabs=np.max(np.abs(res))
          tol=1e-12*maxabs if maxabs>0 else 1e-15
          res[np.abs(res)<tol]=0.0
          return res
       except Exception:
          pass
    return np.convolve(u,v)
```
Using `u.size*v.size` for p_rev conv n*2n. For n=1500 product4.5M > threshold -> FFT. Good.

`local_score`:
```
def local_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size<1: return inf
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if not finite or S<=1e-12: return inf
   c=fast_convolve(a,a)
   M=float(np.max(c))
   if not finite or M<0: M=0 if M>-1e-15 else inf?
   return 2.0*a.size*M/(S*S)
```
If FFT tiny negative max? max will be positive. If all zeros S small ->inf.

`normalize`:
```
def normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return None
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return None
   s=a.sum()
   if not finite or s<=1e-12: return None
   return a/s
```
Evaluator clamps values >1000 but our normalized small; okay.

`resize_sequence`: Use interpolation on positive floor. If a has zeros, floor.
```
def resize_sequence(a,n):
   a=normalize(a)
   if a is None: return None
   if a.size==n: return a
   if n<2: return np.array([1.0])
   old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
   new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # use floor sqrt? If a zero, log problematic; use small floor relative to mean
   floor = 1e-6 / max(a.size,1)
   y = np.maximum(a, floor)
   y = y/y.sum()
   # maybe interpolate log to preserve shape negative logs? Use log with floor.
   ylog=np.log(y)
   v=np.exp(np.interp(new,old,ylog))
   v=np.clip(v,0,1000)
   # This underestimates if floor? okay.
   return normalize(v)
```
If some values very small, log floor large negative; interpolation may create small. Good.
But if length decreases, log interpolation may lose mass at endpoints? okay.

`make_candidate` detailed:
```
def make_candidate(n, seed, kind, parent=None):
   if n<2: n=2
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   xm=x-0.5
   if kind is None: kind=int(rng.integers(0,6))
   kind=int(kind)%6
   if kind==0:
      val=1.0+0.2*rng.standard_normal(n)
   elif kind==1: # asymmetric U
      alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.5,5.0)); beta=float(rng.uniform(-3,3)); gamma=float(rng.uniform(-1,1))
      val=np.exp(alpha*xm*xm + beta*xm + gamma*x)
   elif kind==2: # endpoint gaussians + middle dip/bump
      w1=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.15)); w2=w1*float(rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)); wmid=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.4))
      a1=float(rng.uniform(0.3,2.0)); a2=float(rng.uniform(0.3,2.0)); amid=float(rng.uniform(-0.5,1.0))
      val=0.05 + a1*np.exp(-(x/w1)**2) + a2*np.exp(-((x-1)/w2)**2) + amid*np.exp(-((x-0.5)/wmid)**2)
   elif kind==3:
      if parent is not None:
         return resize_sequence(parent,n)
      val=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0,n)
   elif kind==4:
      val=np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-3,3))*x)
   else:
      # random beta
      val=rng.beta(2,2,n)
   val=np.clip(val,1e-8,10.0)
   # smooth slightly if n>4
   if n>4:
      kern=np.array([0.25,0.5,0.25]); val=np.convolve(val,kern,mode='same')
   val=np.clip(val,1e-8,10.0)
   return normalize(val)
```
Ensure positive. kind 3 parent; but if kind3 used and parent None, uniform.

def official_score_list(seq):

      vals=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.clip(seq,0,1000)])
      return float(vals)
   except Exception:
      return float('inf')
```

Let's detail `multiplicative_update_once` to avoid time. It computes c twice? one for weights and one for local line search. We can compute c once and pass? We can write internal but no nested. Top-level function:
```
def multiplicative_update_once(a, temp, K, rng, top=False):
    a=normalize(a)
    c=fast_convolve(a,a)
    M=max(c); p=peak_weights(c,temp,K,top)
    h=grad_from_weights(a,p)
    hmax=float(np.max(h))
    if not finite or hmax<=0: return a, local_score(a)
    d=(float(np.mean(h))-h)/hmax
    if rng is not None and rng.random()<0.1:
       d += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(a.size)
    best=a; bestsc=local_score(a)
    steps=(0.03,0.07,0.15,0.3,0.55,0.9)
    for st in steps:
       # if st large and d has large negative, exp under/overflow. clip step*d to [-5,5]
       dd=np.clip(st*d, -5.0, 5.0)
       cand=a*np.exp(dd)
       cand=normalize(cand)
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=local_score(cand)
       if sc<bestsc:
          best=cand; bestsc=sc
    return best, bestsc
```

```
def plan_budget(planned_frac, planned_cap, budget, deadline, min_left):
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    alloc = min(planned_frac*float(budget), planned_cap, max(0.0, rem - min_left))
    return alloc
```

Now, code exact for `compute_alloc`:
```
def compute_alloc(frac, cap, budget, deadline, min_left):
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem <= min_left:
        return 0.0
    b = min(frac * float(budget), float(cap))
    b = min(b, rem - min_left)
    if b < 0: b=0.0
    return b
```

```
def softmax_z(z):
    # z length n-1
    n = z.size + 1
    full = np.zeros(n)
    if z.size: full[1:] = z
    m=full.max(); e=np.exp(full-m); return e/e.sum()

</think>

```python
"""
Composite search for low-score nonnegative step-function coefficients.

Objective (as used by evaluate_sequence):
    score = 2*n*max(full_convolution(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

This program combines several complementary local-search ideas:
  - natural-gradient multiplicative updates controlled by active convolution peaks,
  - LP directions minimizing/maximizing cross-convolution with the current sequence,
  - smooth logsumexp L-BFGS-B continuation on a softmax parameterization,
  - stationary Hankel linear-system updates,
  - coarse-to-fine resizing from a parent/best sequence,
  - final selection by the official evaluator when available.

All helper functions are top-level; no lambdas or nested closures are used.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = False
    linprog = None
    minimize = None

try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
    HAVE_FFT = True
except Exception:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
    HAVE_FFT = False

BEST_SEQ = None
BEST_SCORE = float('inf')
SEARCH_POOL = []
SMOOTH_TEMP = 10.0


def normalize(a):
    """Clip to valid nonnegative finite values and normalize sum to 1."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-14:
        return None
    return a / s


def fast_convolve(u, v):
    """Full convolution.  Uses FFT for large products when available."""
    try:
        u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if u.size == 0 or v.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if HAVE_FFT and (u.size * v.size) > 4000000:
        try:
            res = fftconvolve(u, v, mode='full')
            res = np.asarray(res, dtype=np.float64)
            mx = float(np.max(np.abs(res)))
            tol = 1e-12 * mx if mx > 0.0 else 1e-15
            res[np.abs(res) < tol] = 0.0
            return res
        except Exception:
            pass
    return np.convolve(u, v)


def local_score(a):
    """Fast local version of the objective, assuming finite nonnegative values."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    if a.size == 0:
        return float('inf')
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float('inf')
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-14:
        return float('inf')
    c = fast_convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float('inf')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float('inf')
    if m < 0.0:
        if m > -1e-12:
            m = 0.0
        else:
            return float('inf')
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def peak_weights(c, temp, K=None, top=False):
    """Normalize a vector of weights over convolution lags."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    if top or temp is None or temp > 250.0:
        if K is None:
            K = max(1, L // 20)
        K = int(max(1, min(L, K)))
        if K >= L:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
        return p
    u = c / m
    vals = float(temp) * (u - 1.0)
    vals = np.clip(vals, -40.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(vals)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def grad_from_weights(a, p):
    """
    For weighted sum of convolution coefficients, derive
        h_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j}
    which is half the gradient of the weighted sum w.r.t. a_i.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size != (2 * n - 1):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    pr = p[::-1]
    conv = fast_convolve(a, pr)
    start = n - 1
    end = 2 * n - 1
    if conv.size < end:
        conv = np.convolve(a, pr)
    term = conv[start:end][::-1]
    return np.asarray(term, dtype=np.float64)


def local_score_normalized_sum1(a):
    """Score for an array already normalized to sum 1."""
    c = fast_convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float('inf')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float('inf')
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def multiplicative_update_once(a, temp, K, top=False, rng=None):
    """One natural-gradient step with line search."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float('inf')
    n = a.size
    c = fast_convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return a, float('inf')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    base_score = float(2.0 * n * m)
    p = peak_weights(c, temp, K, top)
    h = grad_from_weights(a, p)
    hmax = float(np.max(h))
    if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
        return a, base_score
    d = (float(np.mean(h)) - h) / hmax
    if rng is not None and rng.random() < 0.12:
        d = d + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    best = a
    bestsc = base_score
    if n > 1024:
        steps = (0.10, 0.30)
    elif n > 512:
        steps = (0.08, 0.20, 0.40)
    else:
        steps = (0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.70)
    for st in steps:
        dd = np.clip(st * d, -5.0, 5.0)
        cand = a * np.exp(dd)
        cand = normalize(cand)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = local_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-11:
            best = cand
            bestsc = float(sc)
    return best, bestsc


def multiplicative_optimize(a, budget, seed):
    """Repeated multiplicative updates with adaptive weights and restarts."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None or budget <= 0.0:
        return None, float('inf')
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    bestsc = local_score(best)
    if bestsc == float('inf') or budget <= 0.0:
        return best, bestsc
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)
    temp = 25.0
    K = max(4, n // 18)
    stuck = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        use_top = (it % 3 == 2) or (temp > 220.0)
        cand, sc = multiplicative_update_once(best, temp, K, top=use_top, rng=rng)
        if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
            best = cand
            bestsc = float(sc)
            stuck = 0
            if temp < 320.0:
                temp = min(320.0, temp * 1.04)
            if it % 50 == 0 and K > 2:
                K = max(2, int(K * 0.92))
        else:
            stuck += 1
            if stuck % 10 == 0:
                temp = max(6.0, temp * 0.72)
                K = min(2 * n - 1, max(4, int(K * 1.35)))
            if stuck > 70:
                cand = best * np.exp(0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                cand = normalize(cand)
                if cand is not None:
                    sc2 = local_score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < bestsc:
                        best = cand
                        bestsc = float(sc2)
                else:
                    cand = 0.90 * best + 0.10 * (np.ones(n) / float(n))
                    cand = normalize(cand)
                    if cand is not None:
                        sc2 = local_score(cand)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < bestsc:
                            best = cand
                            bestsc = float(sc2)
                temp = 30.0
                K = max(4, n // 18)
                stuck = 0
        it += 1
    return best, bestsc


def quick_candidate_list(a):
    """Cheap deterministic variants: reversal, uniform mix, endpoint tilts."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return []
    out = [a.copy(), a[::-1].copy()]
    n = a.size
    u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    for t in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
        out.append(normalize((1.0 - t) * a + t * u))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xm = x - 0.5
    for alpha in (-1.5, 1.5):
        w = np.exp(alpha * xm)
        w = np.clip(w, 1e-8, None)
        out.append(normalize(a * (w / float(np.max(w)))))
    last = []
    for cand in out:
        if cand is not None:
            last.append(cand)
    return last


def softmax_log(c, temp):
    """Stable logsumexp(temp*c)/temp and corresponding softmax weights."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float('inf'), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    x = float(temp) * c
    m = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return float('inf'), p
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        mval = float(np.max(c))
        return mval if np.isfinite(mval) else float('inf'), p
    soft = (m + np.log(s)) / float(temp)
    p = e / s
    return float(soft), p


def softmax_z(z):
    """Recover nonnegative sum-one vector from log-ratio parameters z[1:]/z[0]."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size + 1
    full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size:
        full[1:] = z
    m = float(np.max(full))
    e = np.exp(full - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def smooth_value(z):
    """Smooth approximate score and gradient w.r.t. log-ratio parameters."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size + 1
    full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size:
        full[1:] = z
    m = float(np.max(full))
    e = np.exp(full - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float('inf'), np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    a = e / s
    c = fast_convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float('inf'), np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    soft, p = softmax_log(c, SMOOTH_TEMP)
    if not np.isfinite(soft):
        return float('inf'), np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * soft
    h = grad_from_weights(a, p)
    grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * h
    g = grad_a - a * float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    if z.size == 0:
        return val, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    return val, np.asarray(g[1:], dtype=np.float64)


def smooth_fun(z):
    v, _ = smooth_value(z)
    return v


def smooth_jac(z):
    _, g = smooth_value(z)
    return g


def smooth_optimize(a, budget, seed):
    """Smooth logsumexp continuation using L-BFGS-B, with exact line search."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None or budget <= 0.0 or n_invalid(a):
        return a, local_score(a) if a is not None else (None, float('inf'))
    if not HAVE_SCIPY_OPT or minimize is None:
        return a, local_score(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 1024:
        return a, local_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    bestsc = local_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(bestsc):
        return best, bestsc
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)
    if n <= 256:
        temps = (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1000.0)
    else:
        temps = (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0)
    _ = int(seed)  # noqa: F841
    for temp in temps:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 15.0:
            break
        global SMOOTH_TEMP
        SMOOTH_TEMP = float(temp)
        ap = np.clip(best, 1e-12, None)
        ap = normalize(ap)
        if ap is None:
            break
        z0 = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
        if n > 1:
            z0 = np.log(np.maximum(ap[1:], 1e-200) / float(max(ap[0], 1e-200)))
            z0 = np.clip(z0, -30.0, 30.0)
        maxfun = int(max(20, min(180, rem / 2.5)))
        maxit = int(max(5, min(40, maxfun // 2)))
        try:
            opts = {
                'maxiter': maxit,
                'maxfun': maxfun,
                'gtol': 1e-4,
                'ftol': 1e-8,
                'iprint': -1,
            }
            res = minimize(smooth_fun, z0, jac=smooth_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts)
            z1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(z1)):
                continue
            cand = softmax_z(z1)
            cand = normalize(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            for t in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.0):
                mix = normalize((1.0 - t) * best + t * cand)
                if mix is None:
                    continue
                sc = local_score(mix)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-11:
                    best = mix
                    bestsc = float(sc)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return best, bestsc


def n_invalid(a):
    return (a is None) or (np.asarray(a).size < 1)


def lp_min_t(a, seed, timeout=None):
    """
    LP direction: find g>=0, sum g=1 minimizing max_k (a*g)_k.
    Dense for n<=1024.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 1024:
        return None
    try:
        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            A[i:i + n, i] = a
        A[:, n] = -1.0
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c[n] = 1.0
        c[:n] += 1e-9 * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, n)
        Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)
        options = {'presolve': True}
        if timeout is not None and np.isfinite(timeout) and timeout > 1.0:
            options['time_limit'] = float(max(1.0, timeout))
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds,
                          method='highs', options=options)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds,
                          method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
        if res is not None and getattr(res, 'success', False):
            g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
            g = normalize(g)
            if g is not None:
                return g
    except MemoryError:
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def lp_max_sum(a, B, seed, timeout=None):
    """
    SOTA-style LP: maximize sum g with 0<=g<=B and a*g <= current peak.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 1024:
        return None
    c = fast_convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return None
    tau = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
        return None
    try:
        F = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            F[i:i + n, i] = a
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 777)
        obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        obj += 1e-9 * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, n)
        b = np.full(2 * n - 1, tau * (1.0 + 1e-8), dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, float(B))] * n
        options = {'presolve': True}
        if timeout is not None and np.isfinite(timeout) and timeout > 1.0:
            options['time_limit'] = float(max(1.0, timeout))
        try:
            res = linprog(obj, A_ub=F, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=options)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            res = linprog(obj, A_ub=F, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs',
                          options={'presolve': True})
        if res is not None and getattr(res, 'success', False):
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
            g = normalize(g)
            if g is not None:
                return g
    except MemoryError:
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def try_mix(base, target, mix_steps):
    """Line search on convex mixture of two normalized sequences."""
    base = normalize(base)
    target = normalize(target)
    if base is None or target is None:
        return base, local_score(base) if base is not None else (None, float('inf'))
    best = base.copy()
    bestsc = local_score(base)
    for t in mix_steps:
        cand = normalize((1.0 - float(t)) * base + float(t) * target)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = local_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-11:
            best = cand
            bestsc = float(sc)
    return best, bestsc


def lp_optimize(a, budget, seed):
    """Apply LP directions interleaved with multiplicative fallback."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None or budget <= 0.0:
        return None, float('inf')
    if linprob_missing():
        return a, local_score(a)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    bestsc = local_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(bestsc):
        return best, bestsc
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 5151)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)
    max_iters = 10 if n <= 512 else 6
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and stall < 6 and it < max_iters:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        g = None
        if it % 2 == 0:
            g = lp_min_t(best, int(seed) + 17 * it, timeout=min(25.0, rem - 4.0))
            mix_steps = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 1.0)
        else:
            g = lp_max_sum(best, 30.0, int(seed) + 19 * it, timeout=min(25.0, rem - 4.0))
            mix_steps = (0.03, 0.07, 0.13, 0.25, 0.45, 0.70)
        improved = False
        if g is not None:
            if not np.allclose(g, best, rtol=1e-6, atol=1e-12):
                cand, sc = try_mix(best, g, mix_steps)
                if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
                    best = cand
                    bestsc = float(sc)
                    stall = 0
                    improved = True
        if not improved:
            cand, sc = multiplicative_update_once(best, 60.0, max(4, n // 18),
                                                  top=(it % 2 == 1), rng=rng)
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                bestsc = float(sc)
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
        it += 1
    return best, bestsc


def linprob_missing():
    return (linprog is None) or (not HAVE_SCIPY_OPT)


def stationarity_point(p):
    """
    Solve A x = 1, where A_ij = p_{i+j} (with p over convolution lags).
    If a positive solution exists, it is a stationary point for fixed p.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size < 3:
        return None
    n = (p.size + 1) // 2
    if p.size != (2 * n - 1) or n < 2 or n > 512:
        return None
    try:
        idx = np.add.outer(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64), np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64))
        A = p[np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.intp)].copy()
        A += 1e-8 * np.eye(n, dtype=np.float64)
        try:
            x = np.linalg.solve(A, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        except Exception:
            x = np.linalg.lstsq(A, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), rcond=None)[0]
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return None
        neg_frac = float(np.sum(x < 0.0)) / float(n)
        if neg_frac > 0.5:
            return None
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
        x = normalize(x)
        if x is None:
            return None
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return None
        return x
    except Exception:
        return None


def stationarity_optimize(a, budget, seed):
    """Iterative fixed-p stationary solves with exact line search."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None or budget <= 0.0:
        return None, float('inf')
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or n > 512:
        return a, local_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    bestsc = local_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(bestsc):
        return best, bestsc
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 909)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)
    beta = 25.0
    stall = 0
    max_iters = 14 if n <= 256 else 8
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and stall < 10 and it < max_iters:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break
        c = fast_convolve(best, best)
        if c.size > 0:
            p = peak_weights(c, beta, max(4, n // 12), top=(it % 4 == 3))
        else:
            p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        x = stationarity_point(p)
        improved = False
        if x is not None:
            cand, sc = try_mix(best, x, (0.10, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60, 0.85, 1.0))
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                bestsc = float(sc)
                stall = 0
                improved = True
                beta = min(350.0, beta * 1.12)
        else:
            beta = max(6.0, beta * 0.85)
        if not improved:
            cand, sc = multiplicative_update_once(best, beta, max(4, n // 12),
                                                  top=(it % 2 == 1), rng=rng)
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                bestsc = float(sc)
                stall = 0
                beta = max(6.0, beta * 0.90)
            else:
                stall += 1
                beta = max(6.0, beta * 0.85)
        it += 1
    return best, bestsc


def optimize_candidate(a, budget, seed):
    """Run a composite local optimizer for one sequence."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None or budget <= 0.0:
        return None, float('inf')
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a, local_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    bestsc = local_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(bestsc):
        return best, bestsc
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)

    # Very quick deterministic variants.
    for cand in quick_candidate_list(best):
        sc = local_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-11:
            best = cand
            bestsc = float(sc)

    # 1. Multiplicative warm-up.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    b1 = min(0.22 * float(budget), 95.0, max(0.0, rem))
    if b1 > 1.0:
        cand, sc = multiplicative_optimize(best, b1, int(seed) + 11)
        if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
            best = cand
            bestsc = float(sc)

    # 2. LP directions.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if linprob_missing() is False and n <= 1024:
        b2 = min(0.16 * float(budget), 120.0, max(0.0, rem))
        if b2 > 5.0:
            cand, sc = lp_optimize(best, b2, int(seed) + 31)
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                bestsc = float(sc)

    # 3. Smooth L-BFGS-B.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if HAVE_SCIPY_OPT and minimize is not None and n <= 1024:
        b3 = min(0.34 * float(budget), 320.0, max(0.0, rem - 10.0))
        if b3 > 10.0:
            cand, sc = smooth_optimize(best, b3, int(seed) + 61)
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                bestsc = float(sc)

    # 4. Stationary Hankel update.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if n <= 512:
        b4 = min(0.10 * float(budget), 60.0, max(0.0, rem - 5.0))
        if b4 > 3.0:
            cand, sc = stationarity_optimize(best, b4, int(seed) + 91)
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                bestsc = float(sc)

    # 5. Final multiplicative polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 4.0:
        cand, sc = multiplicative_optimize(best, max(1.0, rem - 1.0), int(seed) + 131)
        if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc - 1e-10:
            best = cand
            bestsc = float(sc)

    return best, bestsc


def get_parent_array():
    """Fetch the known previous best sequence, if present."""
    try:
        obj = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
        if obj is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return None
        return arr
    except Exception:
        return None


def resize_sequence(a, n):
    """Resize a sequence to length n by log-space interpolation."""
    a = normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    floor = 1e-7 / float(max(a.size, 1))
    y = np.maximum(a, floor)
    y = normalize(y)
    if y is None:
        return None
    yl = np.log(y)
    vlog = np.interp(new, old, yl)
    v = np.exp(vlog)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize(v)


def make_candidate(n, seed, kind, parent=None):
    """Create a positive initial sequence of length n."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    seed = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if kind is None:
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    kind = int(kind) % 6
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    xm = x - 0.5
    if kind == 3 and parent is not None:
        return resize_sequence(parent, n)
    if kind == 0:
        val = 1.0 + 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 1:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.7, 5.0))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
        gamma = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        val = np.exp(alpha * xm * xm + beta * xm + gamma * x)
    elif kind == 2:
        w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.14))
        w2 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.14))
        wm = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.35))
        a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.5))
        a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.5))
        am = float(rng.uniform(-0.25, 1.2))
        val = 0.50 + a1 * np.exp(-(x / w1) ** 2) + a2 * np.exp(-((x - 1.0) / w2) ** 2)
        val += am * np.exp(-((x - 0.5) / wm) ** 2)
    elif kind == 3:
        if parent is not None:
            return resize_sequence(parent, n)
        val = rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0, n)
    elif kind == 4:
        val = np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0)) * x)
    else:
        val = rng.beta(2.5, 2.5, n)
    val = np.asarray(val, dtype=np.float64)
    val = np.clip(val, 1e-8, 10.0)
    if n > 4:
        kern = np.array([0.25, 0.5, 0.25], dtype=np.float64)
        val = np.convolve(val, kern, mode='same')
        val = np.clip(val, 1e-8, 10.0)
    out = normalize(val)
    if out is None:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out


def official_score(a):
    """Call the official evaluator safely."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return float('inf')
        total = float(np.sum(arr))
        if total <= 0.01:
            return float('inf')
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in arr]))
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')


def consider_sequence(a, score=None):
    """Update global best and search pool."""
    global BEST_SEQ, BEST_SCORE
    arr = normalize(a)
    if arr is None:
        return
    sc = score
    if sc is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = local_score(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < BEST_SCORE:
        BEST_SEQ = arr.copy()
        BEST_SCORE = float(sc)
        print(f"new local best: score={BEST_SCORE:.8f} n={arr.size}", flush=True)
    SEARCH_POOL.append((arr.copy(), float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else float('inf')))
    if len(SEARCH_POOL) > 10:
        SEARCH_POOL.pop(0)


def compute_alloc(frac, cap, budget, deadline, min_left):
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem <= min_left:
        return 0.0
    b = min(float(frac) * float(budget), float(cap))
    b = min(b, rem - float(min_left))
    if b < 0.0:
        b = 0.0
    return b


def choose_final_candidate(parent):
    """Select final sequence by official evaluator, with local fallback."""
    candidates = []
    if BEST_SEQ is not None:
        candidates.append((BEST_SEQ, local_score(BEST_SEQ)))
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append((parent, local_score(parent)))
    uniform = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    candidates.append((uniform, local_score(uniform)))
    for seq, loc in SEARCH_POOL:
        candidates.append((seq, loc))
    best_off = float('inf')
    best_off_seq = None
    for seq, loc in candidates:
        if seq is None:
            continue
        s = normalize(seq)
        if s is None:
            continue
        off = official_score(s)
        if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_off:
            best_off = off
            best_off_seq = s
            print(f"official score candidate: {off:.8f} n={s.size}", flush=True)
    if best_off_seq is not None:
        return best_off_seq, best_off
    best_loc = float('inf')
    best_loc_seq = None
    for seq, loc in candidates:
        if seq is None:
            continue
        s = normalize(seq)
        if s is None:
            continue
        if not np.isfinite(loc):
            loc = local_score(s)
        if np.isfinite(loc) and loc < best_loc:
            best_loc = loc
            best_loc_seq = s
    if best_loc_seq is not None:
        return best_loc_seq, best_loc
    return uniform, local_score(uniform)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):
    """Main entry point.  Returns the best nonnegative sequence found."""
    global BEST_SEQ, BEST_SCORE, SEARCH_POOL
    BEST_SEQ = None
    BEST_SCORE = float('inf')
    SEARCH_POOL = []
    SMOOTH_TEMP = 10.0

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1500.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1500.0
    budget = max(60.0, budget)
    # Leave a tail for official validation.
    deadline = start_time + budget - 25.0
    seed = int(seed)

    parent = get_parent_array()
    if parent is not None:
        considered = normalize(parent)
        if considered is not None:
            consider_sequence(considered)
            print(f"parent local score: {BEST_SCORE:.8f} n={considered.size}", flush=True)

    # A small safe fallback.
    consider_sequence(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0)

    # Base length: 512 when possible, because the known parent is 512.
    base_n = 512
    if parent is not None:
        base_start = resize_sequence(parent, base_n)
    else:
        base_start = make_candidate(base_n, seed, 1, parent)
    if base_start is None:
        base_start = np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(base_n)

    min_left_base = 320.0 if budget >= 900.0 else 120.0
    base_budget = compute_alloc(0.38, 650.0, budget, deadline, min_left_base)
    if base_budget > 8.0:
        cand, sc = optimize_candidate(base_start, base_budget, seed + 1)
        consider_sequence(cand, sc)

    # Alternative asymmetric start at base length.
    alt_budget = compute_alloc(0.10, 150.0, budget, deadline, 260.0 if budget >= 900.0 else 100.0)
    if alt_budget > 8.0:
        alt = make_candidate(base_n, seed + 7, 1, parent)
        p512 = resize_sequence(parent, base_n) if parent is not None else None
        if p512 is not None or BEST_SEQ is not None:
            ref = p512 if p512 is not None else BEST_SEQ
            alt = normalize(0.55 * ref + 0.45 * alt)
            if alt is None:
                alt = make_candidate(base_n, seed + 13, 2, parent)
        cand, sc = optimize_candidate(alt, alt_budget, seed + 17)
        consider_sequence(cand, sc)

    # Upscale to 1024.
    long_budget = compute_alloc(0.26, 380.0, budget, deadline, 180.0 if budget >= 900.0 else 80.0)
    if long_budget > 15.0 and BEST_SEQ is not None:
        cand1k = resize_sequence(BEST_SEQ, 1024)
        if cand1k is not None:
            cand, sc = optimize_candidate(cand1k, long_budget, seed + 113)
            consider_sequence(cand, sc)

    # Upscale to 2048 if time remains.
    very_long_budget = compute_alloc(0.15, 260.0, budget, deadline, 80.0)
    if very_long_budget > 15.0 and BEST_SEQ is not None:
        cand2k = resize_sequence(BEST_SEQ, 2048)
        if cand2k is not None:
            cand, sc = optimize_candidate(cand2k, very_long_budget, seed + 213)
            consider_sequence(cand, sc)

    # Final polish on the global best.
    tail = compute_alloc(0.12, 200.0, budget, deadline, 25.0)
    if tail > 10.0 and BEST_SEQ is not None:
        cand, sc = optimize_candidate(BEST_SEQ, tail, seed + 303)
        consider_sequence(cand, sc)

    final_seq, final_score = choose_final_candidate(parent)
    if final_seq is None:
        final_seq = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) / 128.0
    final_seq = np.clip(np.asarray(final_seq, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_seq.size == 0:
        final_seq = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) / 128.0
    print(f"FINAL selected n={final_seq.size} local/approx score={final_score:.8f}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
